    75	// ****************************************************************************
    76	// Function: fill
    77	//
    78	// Purpose:
    79	//   Initialize array with random values
    80	//
    81	// Arguments:
    82	//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
    83	//   n: number of elements in the array
    84	//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
    85	//
    86	// ****************************************************************************
    87	void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    88	    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
    89	        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    90	    }
    91	}
    92	
    93	// ****************************************************************************
    94	// Function: initRandomMatrix
    95	//
    96	// Purpose:
    97	//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square
    98	//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse
    99	//   row (CSR) format.
   100	//
   101	// Arguments:
   102	//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
   103	//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
   104	//                  last element is the index one past the last element
   105	//   n:             number of nonzero elements
   106	//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
   107	//
   108	// ****************************************************************************
   109	void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
   110	    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;
   111	
   112	    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
   113	    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));
   114	
   115	    // Seed random number generator
   116	    srand(8675309);
   117	
   118	    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
   119	    bool fillRemaining = false;
   120	    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
   121	        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
   122	        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
   123	            const uint64_t numEntriesLeft = static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) * dim -
   124	                                            (static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * dim + j);
   125	            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
   126	            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
   127	                fillRemaining = true;
   128	            }
   129	            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
   130	            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
   131	                // Assign (i,j) a value
   132	                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
   133	                nnzAssigned++;
   134	            }
   135	        }
   136	    }
   137	    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
   138	    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
   139	}
   140	
   141	// ****************************************************************************
   142	// Function: spmvCpu
   143	//
   144	// Purpose:
   145	//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
   146	//
   147	// Arguments:
   148	//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
   149	//   cols: array of column indices for each element
   150	//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
   151	//                  last element is the index one past the last element
   152	//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
   153	//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
   154	//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
   155	//
   156	// ****************************************************************************
   157	void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
   158	             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
   159	    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
   160	        double t = 0.0;
   161	        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
   162	            const auto col = cols[j];
   163	            t += val[j] * vec[col];
   164	        }
   165	        out[i] = t;
   166	    }
   167	}
   168	
   169	// Each cooperative group owns one CSR row.  Selecting the group width from
   170	// the matrix's average row length keeps short rows from wasting a full warp,
   171	// while retaining coalesced matrix loads for longer rows.
   172	template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
   173	__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
   174	void spmvCsrVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
   175	                        const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   176	                        const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   177	                        const double* __restrict__ vec,
   178	                        const index_t dim,
   179	                        double* __restrict__ out) {
   180	    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW == 1 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 2 ||
   181	                  THREADS_PER_ROW == 4 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 8 ||
   182	                  THREADS_PER_ROW == 16 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 32);
   183	
   184	    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
   185	    const size_t globalThread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   186	    const size_t rowNumber = globalThread / THREADS_PER_ROW;
   187	    const bool validRow = rowNumber < dim;
   188	    double sum = 0.0;
   189	
   190	    if (validRow) {
   191	        const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(rowNumber);
   192	        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
   193	        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
   194	        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
   195	             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
   196	            sum += val[element] * __ldg(vec + cols[element]);
   197	        }
   198	    }
   199	
   200	#pragma unroll
   201	    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   202	        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    if (validRow && lane == 0) {
   206	        out[rowNumber] = sum;
   207	    }
   208	}
   209	
   210	// Very long rows benefit from more than one warp.  This kernel assigns an
   211	// entire block to a row, then performs a two-level warp/shared-memory
   212	// reduction.  The matrix generated by this benchmark has balanced rows, so
   213	// no atomics or load-balancing queues are needed.
   214	__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
   215	void spmvCsrBlockKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
   216	                       const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   217	                       const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   218	                       const double* __restrict__ vec,
   219	                       const index_t dim,
   220	                       double* __restrict__ out) {
   221	    constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
   222	    __shared__ double warpSums[WARPS_PER_BLOCK];
   223	
   224	    const index_t row = blockIdx.x;
   225	    if (row >= dim) {
   226	        return;
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    double sum = 0.0;
   230	    const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
   231	    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
   232	    for (index_t element = rowStart + threadIdx.x; element < rowEnd;
   233	         element += blockDim.x) {
   234	        sum += val[element] * __ldg(vec + cols[element]);
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & 31u;
   238	    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x >> 5u;
   239	#pragma unroll
   240	    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   241	        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
   242	    }
   243	    if (lane == 0) {
   244	        warpSums[warp] = sum;
   245	    }
   246	    __syncthreads();
   247	
   248	    if (warp == 0) {
   249	        sum = lane < WARPS_PER_BLOCK ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
   250	#pragma unroll
   251	        for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   252	            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
   253	        }
   254	        if (lane == 0) {
   255	            out[row] = sum;
   256	        }
   257	    }
   258	}
   259	
   260	enum class SpmvKernel {
   261	    Vector1,
   262	    Vector2,
   263	    Vector4,
   264	    Vector8,
   265	    Vector16,
   266	    Vector32,
   267	    Block
   268	};
   269	
   270	SpmvKernel chooseSpmvKernel(const index_t nonzeros, const index_t rows) {
   271	    if (rows == 0) {
   272	        return SpmvKernel::Vector1;
   273	    }
   274	
   275	    const double averageRowLength = static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / rows;
   276	    if (averageRowLength <= 1.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector1;
   277	    if (averageRowLength <= 2.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector2;
   278	    if (averageRowLength <= 4.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector4;
   279	    if (averageRowLength <= 8.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector8;
   280	    if (averageRowLength <= 16.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector16;
   281	    if (averageRowLength <= 2048.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector32;
   282	    return SpmvKernel::Block;
   283	}
   284	
   285	template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
   286	void launchSpmvVector(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
   287	                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
   288	                      const index_t dim, double* out) {
   289	    constexpr unsigned int ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
   290	    const unsigned int blocks = (dim + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
   291	    spmvCsrVectorKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
   292	        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   293	}
   294	
   295	void launchSpmv(const SpmvKernel kernel, const double* val, const index_t* cols,
   296	                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
   297	                const index_t dim, double* out) {
   298	    switch (kernel) {
   299	        case SpmvKernel::Vector1:
   300	            launchSpmvVector<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   301	            break;
   302	        case SpmvKernel::Vector2:
   303	            launchSpmvVector<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   304	            break;
   305	        case SpmvKernel::Vector4:
   306	            launchSpmvVector<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   307	            break;
   308	        case SpmvKernel::Vector8:
   309	            launchSpmvVector<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   310	            break;
   311	        case SpmvKernel::Vector16:
   312	            launchSpmvVector<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   313	            break;
   314	        case SpmvKernel::Vector32:
   315	            launchSpmvVector<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   316	            break;
   317	        case SpmvKernel::Block:
   318	            spmvCsrBlockKernel<<<dim, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
   319	                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   320	            break;
   321	    }
   322	}
   323	
   324	SpmvKernel tuneLongRowKernel(const SpmvKernel initialKernel,
   325	                             const index_t nonzeros,
   326	                             const double* val, const index_t* cols,
   327	                             const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
   328	                             const index_t dim, double* out) {
   329	    // The crossover between one warp and one block per row depends on both
   330	    // row count and GPU generation.  A tiny one-time device-side calibration
   331	    // avoids hard-coding a machine-specific threshold.  Short rows use the
   332	    // deterministic group-width heuristic above because a block cannot win
   333	    // enough work per row to offset its synchronization cost.
   334	    if (dim == 0 || static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / dim < 256.0) {
   335	        return initialKernel;
   336	    }
   337	
   338	    constexpr SpmvKernel CANDIDATES[] = {SpmvKernel::Vector32, SpmvKernel::Block};
   339	    constexpr int CALIBRATION_ITERATIONS = 5;
   340	
   341	    // Give both code paths the same cache/clock warm-up before comparing them.
   342	    for (const SpmvKernel candidate : CANDIDATES) {
   343	        launchSpmv(candidate, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   344	    }
   345	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   346	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   347	
   348	    CudaEvent start;
   349	    CudaEvent end;
   350	    float bestTime = std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
   351	    SpmvKernel bestKernel = initialKernel;
   352	
   353	    for (const SpmvKernel candidate : CANDIDATES) {
   354	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
   355	        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < CALIBRATION_ITERATIONS; ++iteration) {
   356	            launchSpmv(candidate, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
   357	        }
   358	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(end.get()));
   359	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(end.get()));
   360	
   361	        float elapsed = 0.0f;
   362	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, start.get(), end.get()));
   363	        if (elapsed < bestTime) {
   364	            bestTime = elapsed;
   365	            bestKernel = candidate;
   366	        }
   367	    }
   368	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   369	    return bestKernel;
   370	}
   371	
   372	// ****************************************************************************
   373	// Function: verifyResults
   374	//
   375	// Purpose:
   376	//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
   377	//
   378	// Arguments:
   379	//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
   380	//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
   381	//   size: number of elements per vector
   382	//
   383	// ****************************************************************************
   384	bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
   385	    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
   386	        const double ref = reference[i];
   387	        const double res = result[i];
   388	        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
   389	            // For very small values, check absolute error
   390	            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   430	            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   431	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   432	            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   433	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   434	            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   435	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   436	            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
   437	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   438	            validate = true;
   439	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   440	            printResults = true;
   441	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   442	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   443	            return 0;
   444	        } else {
   445	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   446	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   447	            return 1;
   448	        }
   449	    }
   450	
   451	    if (sparsity == 0) {
   452	        fprintf(stderr, "Sparsity must be greater than zero.\n");
   453	        return 1;
   454	    }
   455	
   456	    // CSR uses 32-bit indices, so reject matrices whose nonzero array cannot
   457	    // be represented instead of allowing integer wraparound.
   458	    const uint64_t matrixElements = static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * numRows;
   459	    const uint64_t nItems64 = matrixElements / sparsity;
   460	    if (nItems64 > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
   461	        fprintf(stderr, "The requested matrix has too many nonzero elements for 32-bit CSR indices.\n");
   462	        return 1;
   463	    }
   464	    const index_t nItems = static_cast<index_t>(nItems64);
   465	
   466	    printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
   467	    printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
   468	    printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
   469	    const double sparsePercent = matrixElements == 0
   470	                                     ? 100.0
   471	                                     : 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) /
   472	                                                          static_cast<double>(matrixElements));
   473	    printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", nItems, sparsePercent);
   474	    printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
   475	    printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
   476	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   477	
   478	    // Allocate and initialize data structures
   479	    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values
   480	    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices
   481	    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(static_cast<size_t>(numRows) + 1);  // Row delimiters
   482	    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
   483	    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector
   484	
   485	    printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
   486	    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
   487	    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
   488	    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);
   489	
   490	    // For validation, compute reference solution
   491	    std::vector<double> h_reference;
   492	    if (validate) {
   493	        printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
   494	        h_reference.resize(numRows);
   495	        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), 
   496	                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
   497	    }
   498	
   499	    // Transfer invariant CSR data once.  All allocation, initialization, and
   500	    // PCIe traffic remain outside the measured region, matching the original
   501	    // benchmark's focus on SpMV compute throughput.
   502	    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(nItems);
   503	    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(nItems);
   504	    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_rowDelimiters(static_cast<size_t>(numRows) + 1);
   505	    DeviceBuffer<double> d_vec(numRows);
   506	    DeviceBuffer<double> d_out(numRows);
   507	
   508	    if (nItems != 0) {
   509	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_val.data(), h_val.data(),
   510	                              static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(double),
   511	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   512	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cols.data(), h_cols.data(),
   513	                              static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(index_t),
   514	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   515	    }
   516	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_rowDelimiters.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),
   517	                          (static_cast<size_t>(numRows) + 1) * sizeof(index_t),
   518	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   519	    if (numRows != 0) {
   520	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_vec.data(), h_vec.data(),
   521	                              static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double),
   522	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   523	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_out.data(), 0,
   524	                              static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double)));
   525	    }
   526	
   527	    int device = 0;
   528	    cudaDeviceProp deviceProperties{};
   529	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
   530	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&deviceProperties, device));
   531	    printf("CUDA device: %s\n", deviceProperties.name);
   532	
   533	    SpmvKernel kernel = chooseSpmvKernel(nItems, numRows);
   534	    if (iterations != 0) {
   535	        kernel = tuneLongRowKernel(kernel, nItems, d_val.data(), d_cols.data(),
   536	                                   d_rowDelimiters.data(), d_vec.data(),
   537	                                   numRows, d_out.data());
   538	    }
   539	
   540	    // Warm the CUDA context and instruction/data caches before timing.  SpMV
   541	    // is deterministic here, so this does not change the observable result.
   542	    if (numRows != 0 && iterations != 0) {
   543	        launchSpmv(kernel, d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rowDelimiters.data(),
   544	                   d_vec.data(), numRows, d_out.data());
   545	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   546	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   547	    }
   548	
   549	    // Perform SpMV computation
   550	    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   551	    CudaEvent start;
   552	    CudaEvent end;
   553	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
   554	
   555	    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
   556	        if (numRows != 0) {
   557	            launchSpmv(kernel, d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rowDelimiters.data(),
   558	                       d_vec.data(), numRows, d_out.data());
   559	        }
   560	    }
   561	
   562	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   563	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(end.get()));
   564	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(end.get()));
   565	
   566	    float durationMsFloat = 0.0f;
   567	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&durationMsFloat, start.get(), end.get()));
   568	    const double durationMs = durationMsFloat;
   569	
   570	    if (numRows != 0) {
   571	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_out.data(), d_out.data(),
   572	                              static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double),
   573	                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   574	    }
   575	
   576	    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", durationMs);
   577	    
   578	    // Calculate performance metrics
   579	    const double gflops = durationMs > 0.0
   580	                              ? (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (durationMs * 1.0e6)
   581	                              : 0.0;
   582	    const double avgTime = iterations != 0
   583	                               ? durationMs / static_cast<double>(iterations)
   584	                               : 0.0;
   585	    
   586	    printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
   587	    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   588	    
   589	    // Print results for external validation
   590	    if (printResults) {
   591	        print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
   592	    }
   593	
   594	    // Validation
   595	    if (validate) {
   596	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   597	        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);
   598	
   599	        if (valid) {
   600	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   601	            return 0;
   602	        } else {
   603	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   604	            return 1;
   605	        }
   606	    }
   607	
   608	    return 0;
   609	}

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Report CUDA failures at their point of origin instead of silently producing
+// invalid benchmark results.  There is deliberately no CPU fallback: the
+// benchmark always executes its measured work on a CUDA device.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count = 0) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    DeviceBuffer(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept : data_(std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr)) {}
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(DeviceBuffer&&) = delete;
+
+    T* data() { return data_; }
+    const T* data() const { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
 
+class CudaEvent {
+public:
+    CudaEvent() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&event_)); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(event_); }
+
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+
+    cudaEvent_t get() const { return event_; }
+
+private:
+    cudaEvent_t event_{};
+};

+// Each cooperative group owns one CSR row.  Selecting the group width from
+// the matrix's average row length keeps short rows from wasting a full warp,
+// while retaining coalesced matrix loads for longer rows.
+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                        const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                        const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                        const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                        const index_t dim,
+                        double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW == 1 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 2 ||
+                  THREADS_PER_ROW == 4 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 8 ||
+                  THREADS_PER_ROW == 16 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 32);
+
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const size_t globalThread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t rowNumber = globalThread / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const bool validRow = rowNumber < dim;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    if (validRow) {
+        const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(rowNumber);
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum += val[element] * __ldg(vec + cols[element]);
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (validRow && lane == 0) {
+        out[rowNumber] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+// Very long rows benefit from more than one warp.  This kernel assigns an
+// entire block to a row, then performs a two-level warp/shared-memory
+// reduction.  The matrix generated by this benchmark has balanced rows, so
+// no atomics or load-balancing queues are needed.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrBlockKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                       const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                       const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                       const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                       const index_t dim,
+                       double* __restrict__ out) {
+    constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
+    __shared__ double warpSums[WARPS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= dim) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    for (index_t element = rowStart + threadIdx.x; element < rowEnd;
+         element += blockDim.x) {
+        sum += val[element] * __ldg(vec + cols[element]);
+    }
+
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & 31u;
+    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x >> 5u;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        warpSums[warp] = sum;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (warp == 0) {
+        sum = lane < WARPS_PER_BLOCK ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+enum class SpmvKernel {
+    Vector1,
+    Vector2,
+    Vector4,
+    Vector8,
+    Vector16,
+    Vector32,
+    Block
+};
+
+SpmvKernel chooseSpmvKernel(const index_t nonzeros, const index_t rows) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector1;
+    }
+
+    const double averageRowLength = static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / rows;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 1.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector1;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 2.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector2;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 4.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector4;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 8.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector8;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 16.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector16;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 2048.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector32;
+    return SpmvKernel::Block;
+}
+
+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvVector(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    constexpr unsigned int ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned int blocks = (dim + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrVectorKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const SpmvKernel kernel, const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    switch (kernel) {
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector1:
+            launchSpmvVector<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector2:
+            launchSpmvVector<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector4:
+            launchSpmvVector<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector8:
+            launchSpmvVector<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector16:
+            launchSpmvVector<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector32:
+            launchSpmvVector<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Block:
+            spmvCsrBlockKernel<<<dim, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+    }
+}
+
+SpmvKernel tuneLongRowKernel(const SpmvKernel initialKernel,
+                             const index_t nonzeros,
+                             const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                             const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                             const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    // The crossover between one warp and one block per row depends on both
+    // row count and GPU generation.  A tiny one-time device-side calibration
+    // avoids hard-coding a machine-specific threshold.  Short rows use the
+    // deterministic group-width heuristic above because a block cannot win
+    // enough work per row to offset its synchronization cost.
+    if (dim == 0 || static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / dim < 256.0) {
+        return initialKernel;
+    }
+
+    constexpr SpmvKernel CANDIDATES[] = {SpmvKernel::Vector32, SpmvKernel::Block};
+    constexpr int CALIBRATION_ITERATIONS = 5;
+
+    // Give both code paths the same cache/clock warm-up before comparing them.
+    for (const SpmvKernel candidate : CANDIDATES) {
+        launchSpmv(candidate, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent end;
+    float bestTime = std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
+    SpmvKernel bestKernel = initialKernel;
+
+    for (const SpmvKernel candidate : CANDIDATES) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < CALIBRATION_ITERATIONS; ++iteration) {
+            launchSpmv(candidate, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(end.get()));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(end.get()));
+
+        float elapsed = 0.0f;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, start.get(), end.get()));
+        if (elapsed < bestTime) {
+            bestTime = elapsed;
+            bestKernel = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    return bestKernel;
+}

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+// Report CUDA failures at their point of origin instead of silently producing
+// invalid benchmark results.  There is deliberately no CPU fallback: the
+// benchmark always executes its measured work on a CUDA device.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count = 0) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    DeviceBuffer(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept : data_(std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr)) {}
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(DeviceBuffer&&) = delete;
+
+    T* data() { return data_; }
+    const T* data() const { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
 
+class CudaEvent {
+public:
+    CudaEvent() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&event_)); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(event_); }
+
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+
+    cudaEvent_t get() const { return event_; }
+
+private:
+    cudaEvent_t event_{};
+};

     }
 }
 
+// Each cooperative group owns one CSR row.  Selecting the group width from
+// the matrix's average row length keeps short rows from wasting a full warp,
+// while retaining coalesced matrix loads for longer rows.
+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                        const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                        const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                        const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                        const index_t dim,
+                        double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW == 1 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 2 ||
+                  THREADS_PER_ROW == 4 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 8 ||
+                  THREADS_PER_ROW == 16 || THREADS_PER_ROW == 32);
+
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    const size_t globalThread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t rowNumber = globalThread / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const bool validRow = rowNumber < dim;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    if (validRow) {
+        const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(rowNumber);
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd;
+             element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum += val[element] * __ldg(vec + cols[element]);
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+    }
+
+    if (validRow && lane == 0) {
+        out[rowNumber] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+// Very long rows benefit from more than one warp.  This kernel assigns an
+// entire block to a row, then performs a two-level warp/shared-memory
+// reduction.  The matrix generated by this benchmark has balanced rows, so
+// no atomics or load-balancing queues are needed.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrBlockKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                       const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                       const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                       const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                       const index_t dim,
+                       double* __restrict__ out) {
+    constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
+    __shared__ double warpSums[WARPS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= dim) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    for (index_t element = rowStart + threadIdx.x; element < rowEnd;
+         element += blockDim.x) {
+        sum += val[element] * __ldg(vec + cols[element]);
+    }
+
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & 31u;
+    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x >> 5u;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        warpSums[warp] = sum;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (warp == 0) {
+        sum = lane < WARPS_PER_BLOCK ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+enum class SpmvKernel {
+    Vector1,
+    Vector2,
+    Vector4,
+    Vector8,
+    Vector16,
+    Vector32,
+    Block
+};
+
+SpmvKernel chooseSpmvKernel(const index_t nonzeros, const index_t rows) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector1;
+    }
+
+    const double averageRowLength = static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / rows;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 1.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector1;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 2.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector2;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 4.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector4;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 8.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector8;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 16.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector16;
+    if (averageRowLength <= 2048.0) return SpmvKernel::Vector32;
+    return SpmvKernel::Block;
+}
+
+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
+void launchSpmvVector(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    constexpr unsigned int ROWS_PER_BLOCK = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const unsigned int blocks = (dim + ROWS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / ROWS_PER_BLOCK;
+    spmvCsrVectorKernel<THREADS_PER_ROW><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const SpmvKernel kernel, const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    switch (kernel) {
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector1:
+            launchSpmvVector<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector2:
+            launchSpmvVector<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector4:
+            launchSpmvVector<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector8:
+            launchSpmvVector<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector16:
+            launchSpmvVector<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector32:
+            launchSpmvVector<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Block:
+            spmvCsrBlockKernel<<<dim, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+    }
+}
+
+SpmvKernel tuneLongRowKernel(const SpmvKernel initialKernel,
+                             const index_t nonzeros,
+                             const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                             const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                             const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    // The crossover between one warp and one block per row depends on both
+    // row count and GPU generation.  A tiny one-time device-side calibration
+    // avoids hard-coding a machine-specific threshold.  Short rows use the
+    // deterministic group-width heuristic above because a block cannot win
+    // enough work per row to offset its synchronization cost.
+    if (dim == 0 || static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / dim < 256.0) {
+        return initialKernel;
+    }
+
+    constexpr SpmvKernel CANDIDATES[] = {SpmvKernel::Vector32, SpmvKernel::Block};
+    constexpr int CALIBRATION_ITERATIONS = 5;
+
+    // Give both code paths the same cache/clock warm-up before comparing them.
+    for (const SpmvKernel candidate : CANDIDATES) {
+        launchSpmv(candidate, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent end;
+    float bestTime = std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
+    SpmvKernel bestKernel = initialKernel;
+
+    for (const SpmvKernel candidate : CANDIDATES) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < CALIBRATION_ITERATIONS; ++iteration) {
+            launchSpmv(candidate, val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(end.get()));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(end.get()));
+
+        float elapsed = 0.0f;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, start.get(), end.get()));
+        if (elapsed < bestTime) {
+            bestTime = elapsed;
